Abstract: We present a non-paraxial design strategy based on the full ideal aberration-free phase function for a dual-layer rotational zoom metalens. By adopting the exact lens phase expression rather than the conventional parabolic approximation, this approach preserves the higher-order phase contributions critical to large-NA operation. Zooming is achieved by engineering complementary phase distributions on two metasurface layers and dynamically modulating their combined phase via controlled rotation. To realize the target phase profiles, a dielectric metasurface operating at 10 GHz is designed using ceramic cylindrical resonators arranged in a triangular lattice. A systematic parametric optimization of lattice period, cylinder diameter, and height ensures a complete 2pi phase coverage while maintaining a transmission above 85%. The resulting phase-geometry mapping establishes a reliable unit-cell library for device implementation. Leveraging this library, the dual-layer metalens is designed and its performance evaluated through finite-difference time-domain simulations. A prototype with a 300 mm aperture is fabricated and experimentally characterized using three-dimensional near-field scanning measurements. Both simulations and experiments confirm that continuous focal-length tuning is achieved as the rotation angle varies from -60deg to 60deg, corresponding to a focal-length range of 100 to 300 mm and a zoom ratio of 3:1. The device achieves a maximum NA of 0.83, and the measured focal spots remain nearly diffraction-limited. The experimentally extracted focal-length variation agrees closely with theoretical predictions and simulations.

</details>


### [98] [Non-Hermitian photonic time quasicrystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24327)
*Shuhang Chen,Zhi Zheng,Qi Zhu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文引入非厄米Aubry-André-Harper模型的时间类比，发现复化坐标下体放大率被组织为整数相，其斜率由Avila加速数（整数）锁定，建立准周期算子理论与可观测光学响应之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期算子理论中的全局整数（如Avila加速数）通常是抽象数学量，缺乏直接可观测的物理对应。光子时间晶体中的时间调制可放大波，为在光学系统中实现并探测这类全局整数提供了理想平台。

Method: 研究非厄米Aubry-André-Harper模型的时间类比，将非公度时间调制延拓到复相位平面，考察体放大率随复化坐标的变化；分析时间依赖介电常数调制中缺陷对该量子化响应的影响；并以波层面的数值分析考察不同整数相输入频率的放大差异。

Result: 体放大率随复化坐标被组织为不同整数相，每相内斜率锁定为整数，即Avila加速数；调制缺陷在保持解析相结构时量子化响应依然稳健；属于不同整数相的两个输入频率获得指数分离的放大，实现频谱选择并重塑时间拍频图案。

Conclusion: 准周期算子理论中的全局整数可支配非厄米光子时间准晶体中可调、量子化的增长响应，为将抽象算子理论整数与可观测光学传播响应相连接提供了新途径。

Abstract: Photonic time crystals amplify waves through temporal modulation. We study a temporal analogue of the non-Hermitian Aubry-André-Harper model. By continuing an incommensurate temporal modulation into the complex phase plane, we find that the bulk amplification rate as a function of the complexification coordinate is organized into distinct integer phases, with its slope locked to an integer within each phase. This integer is Avila's acceleration, connecting a global structure of quasiperiodic operator theory with a directly observable optical propagation response. The quantized response persists in the presence of defects in the time-dependent permittivity modulation that preserve the underlying analytic phase structure. As a wave-level consequence, two input frequencies belonging to different integer phases acquire exponentially separated amplification, producing spectral selection and reshaping the temporal beat pattern. Our results establish that a global integer of quasiperiodic operator theory can govern a tunable, quantized growth response in a non-Hermitian photonic time quasicrystal.

</details>


### [99] [Broadband Single-Particle Absorption Circular Dichroism Reveals Chiroptical Heterogeneity in Gold Helicoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24772)
*Rohit B. Raj,Susanna Bertuletti,Jeong Hyun Han,Sepin Cho,Debapriya Pal,Nick Feldman,Ki Tae Nam,Willem L. Noorduin,A. Femius Koenderink,Erik C. Garnett*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出一种波长可调的积分球显微镜，通过直接光学能量平衡测量单颗粒在左/右旋圆偏振光下的吸收率，实现宽带吸收圆二色性（CD）及吸收不对称因子 gabs 的单颗粒测量，并揭示手性金螺旋体中显著的颗粒级异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单颗粒水平的吸收型圆二色性定量测量对于理解单个手性纳米结构如何耗散光能至关重要，但宽带吸收率（absorptance）测量在实验上仍具挑战性，而现有集合平均测量会掩盖颗粒特异的响应。

Method: 搭建波长可调的积分球显微镜，在右旋与左旋圆偏振照明下通过直接光学能量平衡确定单个纳米颗粒的吸收率，从而宽带测量 gabs；以无手性金纳米球和强各向异性金纳米棒建立表观吸收CD基线并评估线二色性向圆二色性的泄漏；随后测量由L-谷胱甘肽合成的87个手性金螺旋体（L-helicoids）与由D-谷胱甘肽合成的96个（D-helicoids）。

Result: L-与D-helicoid两群体呈现符号相反的颗粒级平均 gabs，且差异具统计显著性；但单个颗粒在响应符号、幅度、光谱位置及线型上表现出显著异质性，每个群体中超过三分之一的颗粒吸收CD响应符号与群体平均趋势相反。对L群体的相关性SEM分析表明，在具有典型投影螺旋形貌的孤立颗粒中仍存在反号响应，说明聚集和投影形貌的总体差异不足以解释该异质性。

Conclusion: 宽带单颗粒吸收CD光谱学可作为吸收型手性光学异质性的直接探针，揭示被集合平均所掩盖的颗粒特异响应。

Abstract: Quantitative measurements of absorption circular dichroism (CD) at the single-particle level are essential for understanding how individual chiral nanostructures dissipate optical energy, yet broadband absorptance measurements remain experimentally challenging. Here, we introduce a wavelength-tunable integrating-sphere microscope that determines the absorptance of individual nanoparticles under right- and left-circularly polarized illumination through direct optical energy balance, enabling broadband measurement of the absorption dissymmetry factor, gabs. Measurements of achiral gold nanospheres and strongly anisotropic gold nanorods establish the apparent absorption CD baseline and demonstrate minimal detectable linear-dichroism-to-circular-dichroism (LD-to-CD) leakage under the experimental conditions. Applying this approach to 87 chiral gold helicoids synthesized using L-glutathione (L-helicoids) and 96 synthesized using D-glutathione (D-helicoids) reveals mean particle-level gabs values of opposite sign, with a statistically significant difference between the two populations. Individual particles nevertheless exhibit pronounced heterogeneity in response sign, magnitude, spectral position, and line shape. More than one-third of the particles in each population display opposite-sign absorption CD responses relative to their population-average tendency. Correlative SEM analysis of the L-helicoid population further shows that opposite-sign responses persist among isolated particles exhibiting the characteristic projected helicoid morphology, indicating that aggregation and gross differences in projected morphology are insufficient to explain the observed heterogeneity. These results establish broadband single-particle absorption CD spectroscopy as a direct probe of absorptive chiroptical heterogeneity and reveal particle-specific responses obscured by ensemble averaging.

</details>


### [100] [Ultralow-power, high-speed programmable Si photonic circuits with InGaAsP membrane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24611)
*Tomohiro Akazawa,Rui Tang,Hanzhi Tang,Makoto Okano,Yangyang Wan,Nobuyuki Matsuda,Kasidit Toprasertpong,Shinichi Takagi,Mitsuru Takenaka*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出基于InGaAsP/Si混合MOS移相器的可编程光子电路，同时实现超低功耗、高速、高效率、低损耗与可忽略热串扰，并演示了开关与可编程酉变换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程光子电路在光通信、AI计算与量子信息处理中前景广阔，但其扩展受核心器件光移相器制约。现有移相技术在功耗、速度、调制效率、光损耗与热串扰之间存在固有权衡，难以实现高性能大规模集成。

Method: 采用InGaAsP/Si混合金属-氧化物-半导体（MOS）移相器，将MOS电容的低漏电流与InGaAsP膜的强载流子折射率调制相结合；并将其集成到可编程马赫-曾德尔干涉仪网格中进行光开关与可编程酉变换演示。

Result: 静态功耗低于30 fW/π，开关时间555 ps，相位调制效率VπL为0.13 Vcm，载流子引入的额外插损仅0.20 dB/π；集成移相器在电路级保持飞瓦级静态功耗，且热串扰可忽略。

Conclusion: 该工作为可扩展、高性能的可编程光子系统奠定基础，可服务于下一代信号处理与计算。

Abstract: Programmable photonic circuits have emerged as a promising platform for applications ranging from optical communications to artificial-intelligence computing and quantum information processing, but their scaling is fundamentally constrained by their essential building block, the optical phase shifter. Existing phase-shifter technologies face inherent trade-offs among power consumption, operating speed, modulation efficiency, optical loss, and thermal crosstalk, making it challenging to realize high-performance, large-scale programmable photonic circuits. Here, we present a programmable photonic circuit based on InGaAsP/Si hybrid metal-oxide-semiconductor (MOS) phase shifters that combines ultralow power consumption, high-speed operation, high modulation efficiency, low optical loss and negligible thermal crosstalk. The phase shifters combine the low leakage current of a MOS capacitor with the strong carrier-induced refractive-index modulation of an InGaAsP membrane, achieving a static power consumption below 30 fW/$π$, a switching time of 555 ps, a phase-modulation efficiency ($V_πL$) of 0.13 Vcm and a carrier-induced excess insertion loss of only 0.20 dB/$π$. We integrate these phase shifters into a programmable Mach-Zehnder interferometer mesh and demonstrate optical switching and programmable unitary transformations, while maintaining femtowatt-level static power consumption across integrated phase shifters. We further demonstrate circuit-level operation with negligible thermal crosstalk, addressing a major obstacle to densely integrated programmable photonic circuits. These results establish a foundation for scalable, high-performance programmable photonic systems for next-generation signal processing and computation.

</details>


### [101] [Replica Symmetry Breaking in the Quasi Mode Locked Regime of Titanium Sapphire Lasers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24643)
*Nicolas P. Alves,Ricardo V. M. de Almeida Filho,Isaac C. Nunes,Gleison S. Bezerra,Cid B. de Araújo,Marcio H. G. de Miranda,André C. A. Siqueira*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文在钛蓝宝石激光器中观测到准锁模（QML）区的复本对称破缺（RSB），表明QML与RSB的关联可能是锁模激光器的普遍特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前RSB（玻璃态行为）仅在镱基锁模光纤激光器的QML区被观测到，尚不清楚这是特定实现的特例，还是锁模系统的普遍性质。

Method: 通过Parisi重叠与Pearson相关系数分析光谱强度涨落的统计关联，对比研究两套钛蓝宝石激光系统（自制腔与商用激光器），涵盖ASE、SML与QML三种运行区。

Result: 两套钛蓝宝石激光器在QML区均出现清晰的RSB特征，而在ASE和标准锁模（SML）区恢复为复本对称行为。

Conclusion: RSB在钛蓝宝石激光器中得到证实，支持将QML区解释为光子玻璃相；结合此前镱基光纤激光器的结果，QML动力学与RSB的关联可能是锁模激光系统的普遍特征。

Abstract: Recent observations of replica symmetry breaking (RSB) in ytterbium-based mode-locked fiber lasers have revealed glassy behavior in the quasi-mode-locked (QML) regime. Here, we investigate whether this behavior is restricted to a specific laser implementation or represents a more general feature of mode-locked systems. By analyzing statistical correlations of spectral intensity fluctuations through the Parisi overlap and Pearson correlation parameters, we study two Titanium:sapphire laser systems: a homemade cavity and a commercial laser. In both cases, clear signatures of RSB are observed in the QML regime, while replica-symmetric behavior is recovered in the amplified spontaneous emission (ASE) and standard mode-locked (SML) regimes. These results demonstrate the occurrence of RSB in Titanium:sapphire lasers and support the interpretation of the QML regime as a photonic glassy phase. Together with previous observations in ytterbium-based fiber lasers, these findings suggest that the connection between QML dynamics and RSB may be a general feature of mode-locked laser systems.

</details>


### [102] [Real-Time Doppler-Frequency Measurement and Frequency-Pulling Suppression in Arm-locking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24686)
*Zheng-Rong Xiang,Jun Ke,Tian-Chi Zhu,Hao-Xin Xu,Cheng-Gang Shao,Jie Luo*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出基于线性二次高斯（LQG）控制器的实时多普勒频率测量与频率牵引抑制方法，采用时变控制策略在噪声抑制与牵引抑制间取得平衡，实现臂锁定快速捕获。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 臂锁定是空间引力波探测中抑制激光频率噪声的重要技术，但更强的噪声抑制会增大频率牵引幅度，从而延长锁定捕获时间并增加系统不稳定风险，需要兼顾两者的新方法。

Method: 提出基于LQG控制器的实时多普勒频率测量与频率牵引抑制方案，并采用时变控制策略：初期以快速限制频率牵引为主，随后逐步放宽牵引抑制水平、增强激光频率噪声抑制能力，使残余频率噪声在过渡结束时满足TDI要求；对多种臂锁定配置进行综合性能仿真。

Result: 所提抑制器可在约1000 s内降低频率牵引，且将其幅度限制在100 MHz以下，从而免除传统多普勒频率预估计阶段；采用法布里-珀罗激光器时，闭环激光频率噪声的幅度谱密度（ASD）在闭环运行2000 s和9200 s后分别满足第二代和第一代TDI要求。

Conclusion: 该抑制器兼具快速频率牵引抑制与高水平激光频率噪声抑制能力，为臂锁定的快速捕获与锁定提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Arm-locking is an important technique for suppressing laser frequency noise in space-based gravitational-wave detection. However, stronger noise suppression inevitably increases the magnitude of frequency pulling, thereby prolonging the lock-acquisition time and increasing the risk of system instability. To address this issue, this paper proposes a real-time Doppler-frequency measurement and frequency-pulling suppression method based on a linear-quadratic-gaussian controller. To achieve a balance between noise suppression and pulling suppression, a time-varying control strategy is adopted. At the initial stage of the time-varying control process, the primary objective is to rapidly limit frequency pulling. Subsequently, the level of pulling suppression is gradually relaxed while the capability for laser frequency-noise suppression is progressively enhanced, so that the residual frequency noise satisfies the time-delay interferometry (TDI) requirement by the end of the transition. Comprehensive performance simulations are conducted for several arm-locking configurations. The results show that the proposed suppressor can reduce frequency pulling within approximately 1000 s while limiting its magnitude to below 100 MHz, thereby eliminating the need for the conventional Doppler frequency pre-estimation stage. When a Fabry-Perot laser is used, the amplitude spectral density (ASD) of the closed-loop laser frequency noise meets the requirements of second-generation and first-generation TDI after 2000 s and 9200 s of closed-loop operation, respectively. These results demonstrate that the proposed suppressor provides both rapid frequency-pulling suppression and a high level of laser frequency-noise suppression. This new method provide a feasible way for rapid acquisition and locking of arm-locking.

</details>


### [103] [Observation of Replica Symmetry Breaking in Filamentation and Multifilamentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24712)
*André C. A. Siqueira,Guillermo Palacios,Jessica E. Q. Bautista,Anderson M. Amaral,Albert S. Reyna,Edilson L. Falcão-Filho,Cid B. de Araújo*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 实验观测到飞秒激光在蓝宝石与蒸馏水中成丝时横截面强度分布存在复本对称破缺（RSB）现象，将成丝过程与自旋玻璃物理联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有报道指出多丝化由自旋玻璃模型支配，但RSB在单丝生成及伴随锥形辐射的成丝中是否存在尚不明确，需通过实验验证光在超快非线性光学中的类玻璃态行为。

Method: 以800 nm飞秒激光激发蓝宝石晶体与蒸馏水，分析脉冲的横向强度分布来表征RSB；其物理机制为自聚焦与等离子体散焦之间的竞争，多光子激发产生的等离子体引起非线性折射率的局部涨落，进而导致模式间的阻挫。

Result: 实验确认了类玻璃态光子态不仅存在于多丝化中，也存在于单丝生成以及伴随锥形辐射的成丝过程中。

Conclusion: 研究结果建立了统计非线性光学与磁学之间的联系，凸显了超快光现象中光的类玻璃态行为，深化了对统计非线性光学的理解。

Abstract: We report the experimental observation and characterization of Replica Symmetry Breaking (RSB) manifestation while analyzing the transverse intensity profile of pulses in filamentation experiments using sapphire crystal and distilled water, excited by a femtosecond laser centered at 800 nm. The RSB arises from the competition between self-focusing and plasma defocusing, subject to local fluctuations in the nonlinear refractive index generated by plasma via multiphoton excitation, which subsequently promotes frustration among modes. Our results confirm the existence of glassy-like photonic states not only in multifilamentation, as previously reported [W. Ettoumi, J. Kasparian, and J. Wolf, "Spin-glass model governs laser multiple filamentation," Phys. Rev. Lett., vol. 115, no. 3, pp. 033902, 2015], but also in the generation of a single filament and in filamentation accompanied by conical emission. These findings improve the understanding of statistical nonlinear optics by establishing connections with magnetism and highlighting the glassy-like behavior of light in the context of ultrafast optical phenomena.

</details>


### [104] [Phase fields in momentum space of photonic crystal slabs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24728)
*Chuanlin Li,Aobo Ren,Jiang Wu*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文综述了光子晶体板中动量空间相位场（相位涡旋、相位梯度、相位差及多维合成相位场）的产生机制与研究进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动量空间相位调制相较实空间调制具有不受结构中心约束、模式容量无限、具备本征拓扑保护等优势，可实现无需严格光对准的大量独立控制通道，推动高性能高集成光学系统发展；光子晶体板成为研究动量空间相位场的重要平台，故有必要系统梳理该领域进展。

Method: 基于偏振正交分解与时域耦合模理论中的散射矩阵，系统阐述光子晶体板中二维动量空间相位场（相位涡旋、相位梯度、相位差）及多维合成动量空间相位场的产生机制，并综述相关研究与应用进展。

Result: 阐明了光子晶体板中二维及多维合成动量空间相位场的生成机理，并梳理了该方向近期的研究与应用成果。

Conclusion: 总结了动量空间相位场领域的发展现状、优势及可能的突破方向，并对未来工作进行了展望。

Abstract: Optical phase modulation is of great significant importance in fields such as optical communication, information processing, and precision measurement. Compared with real-space modulation, momentum-space phase modulation has distinct advantages: it is free from structural center constraints, supports unlimited mode capacity, and possesses intrinsic topological protection. This inherent flexibility and scalability allow practical applications systems to operate without stringent optical alignment while providing a large number of independent control channels, thereby advancing the development of high-performance, highly integrated optical systems. Photonic crystal slabs, with their open boundary periodicity and capabilities for momentum-space optical field manipulation, have become a crucial platform for research on momentum-space phase fields. Based on polarization orthogonal decomposition and the scattering matrix within temporal coupled-mode theory, this paper systematically elucidates the generation mechanisms of both two-dimensional momentum-space phase fields, including phase vortices, phase gradients, and phase difference, and multidimensional synthetic momentum-space phase fields in photonic crystal slabs, and reviews recent research and application progress in this area. Finally, the development status, advantages, and possible breakthroughs in the field of momentum-space phase fields are summarized and prospects for future work are discussed.

</details>


### [105] [Non-Hermitian mode locking in an L-band monolithic InP dual-ring laser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24798)
*Xiao Sun,Mohanad Al Rubaiee,Jue Wang,John H. Marsh,Stephen J. Sweeney,Anthony E. Kelly,Lianping Hou*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文在单片InP双环激光器中通过电学独立偏置实现无饱和吸收体的电控锁模，输出7.9 ps/18.11 GHz脉冲，并在集成可饱和吸收体时将脉宽降至3.1 ps、集成时间抖动由9.06 ps降至2.46 ps。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合激光腔可通过增益、损耗与模式杂化调控光学模式，为可控锁模与超模工作提供途径；但如何在单片集成平台上以电学方式实现锁模、并兼顾高重复频率皮秒脉冲产生，仍待系统验证。

Method: 采用L波段单片InP双环激光器，无需专用可饱和吸收体，对两环独立偏置以切换单模发射、锁模与杂化超模工作；建立耦合腔模型分析差分非线性失谐对模增益损耗对比的调制，并进行时域仿真；另在集成可饱和吸收体的器件中施加反向偏压进行对比测试。

Result: 杂化超模区测得模间拍频542 MHz；激光输出7.9 ps脉冲、重复频率18.11 GHz；时域仿真所得脉宽与光谱宽度接近实验值；含可饱和吸收体器件在反向偏压下获得3.1 ps脉冲，集成时间抖动由9.06 ps降至2.46 ps。

Conclusion: 结果将耦合腔状态的电学调控与皮秒脉冲产生联系起来，验证了单片InP半导体平台用于高重复频率光通信与微波光子学的可行性。

Abstract: Coupling between laser cavities provides a means of controlling optical modes through their gain, loss, and hybridization. Here we demonstrate electrically controlled mode locking in a monolithic InP dual ring laser operating in the L band without a dedicated saturable absorber. Independent biasing of the two rings accesses single mode emission, mode locking, and hybridized supermode operation, with a measured intermode beat frequency of 542 MHz in the latter regime. The laser generates 7.9 ps pulses at 18.11 GHz. A coupled cavity model describes how differential nonlinear detuning modifies the modal gain loss contrast, and time domain simulations yield pulse durations and spectral widths close to those measured experimentally. In a device incorporating saturable absorbers, reverse bias enables 3.1 ps pulses and reduces the integrated timing jitter from 9.06 to 2.46 ps relative to the unbiased condition. These results connect electrical control of coupled-cavity states with picosecond pulse generation in a monolithic InP semiconductor platform for high repetition rate optical communications and microwave photonics.

</details>


### [106] [Collective cavity quantum electrodynamics in solid-state optical clocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24800)
*Karen Mamian,Georgy A. Kazakov,Thorsten Schumm,Charles Roques-Carmes*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文针对固态频率标准受强退相干限制的问题，提出并分析了三种基于腔QED增强的$^{229}$Th核钟询问方案，利用钍核与纳米光子模式的集体耦合将相干时间与辐射寿命的时间尺度失配转化为优势，并揭示集体协同性对钟频率不稳定度的决定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态频率标准通常受强退相干限制，导致传统询问方案效率低下。$^{229}$Th核钟为固态光学计量与核腔QED提供了独特平台，但其当前实验中相干时间比辐射寿命短很多个数量级，存在显著的时间尺度失配，需要新的询问策略。

Method: 提出并分析三种腔QED增强的钟询问方案；利用钍核与纳米光子模式的集体耦合，实现快速询问与探测；分析集体协同性在决定钟频率不稳定度中的核心作用；推导钟运行的最优条件（功率、工作点失谐、钍密度）。

Result: 所提方案能够将相干时间与辐射寿命之间的时间尺度失配转化为优势，在长布居寿命下仍可实现快速询问与检测；集体协同性是决定钟频率不稳定度的关键因素；给出了功率、工作点失谐与钍密度等最优运行条件。

Conclusion: 腔QED增强的询问方案为固态$^{229}$Th核钟提供了可行路径，通过集体协同性优化可在强退相干环境下实现高效询问，并为固态光学计量与核腔QED应用奠定基础。

Abstract: Solid-state frequency standards are generally limited by strong decoherence, rendering conventional interrogation schemes inefficient. The $^{229}$Th nuclear clock provides a unique and timely platform for solid-state optical metrology and nuclear cavity quantum electrodynamics (QED), featuring a coherence time many orders of magnitude shorter than the radiative lifetime in current experiments. Here, we propose and analyze three cavity QED-enhanced clock interrogation schemes that turn this timescale mismatch into an advantage, leveraging collective coupling of thorium nuclei to nanophotonic modes to enable fast interrogation and detection despite the long population lifetime. We reveal the central role of collective cooperativity in determining the clock frequency instability, and derive the optimal conditions (power, working-point detuning, thorium density) for clock operation.

</details>


### [107] [Fisher-information training of optical sensing front ends from natural fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24915)
*Abhinav Sinha,Kai Wang*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 本文提出无需响应模型或参数标注测量即可进行可编程光学前端原位优化的方法：利用自然参数涨落作为训练信号，从测量协方差中减去条件探测噪声后构造单参数FI与多参数先验加权FI谱的代理，仿真光子计数实验证明该方法可实现接近量子极限的模式分类器，并支持无需仪器响应模型的计数更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fisher信息驱动的可编程光学前端原位优化通常依赖响应模型或参数标注测量，这在实际中难以获得或代价高昂。作者希望探索能否利用系统中自然存在的参数涨落本身作为训练信号，从而摆脱对受控参数扫描和精确响应模型的依赖，实现自适应光学测量。

Method: 在局部仿射响应假设下，从测量协方差中减去条件探测噪声，构造单参数Fisher信息的代理量以及多参数的先验加权FI谱代理量。以两个非相干点源的模拟光子计数为数据，训练模式分类器；并演示仅基于计数的梯度更新方式，无需任何仪器响应模型。

Result: 训练所得模式分类器在分离度估计上逼近量子极限，在联合质心—分离度估计上逼近Nagaoka–Hayashi单份本界；仅计数更新在无响应模型条件下达到与基于模型的梯度相当的性能。

Conclusion: 自然参数涨落可作为有效的训练信号，替代受控参数扫描与响应模型，为无需仪器响应模型的自适应光学测量提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Fisher-information-based optimization of programmable optical front ends in situ generally requires a response model or parameter-labeled measurements. We show that natural parameter fluctuations can instead provide the training signal. We construct proxies for the Fisher information (FI) of one parameter and the prior-weighted FI spectrum of multiple parameters from measured covariances after subtracting conditional detection noise, assuming locally affine responses. We use simulated photon counts from two incoherent point sources to train a mode sorter that approaches the quantum limit for separation and the Nagaoka--Hayashi single-copy bound for joint centroid--separation estimation. We demonstrate count-only updates that require no instrument response model and achieve performance comparable to model-based gradients. Our results provide a route to adaptive optical measurements without controlled parameter scans.

</details>

### [108] [Vanadium doping induced valley asymmetries in WS$_2$ monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22580)
*Frederico B. Sousa,Boyang Zheng,Elizabeth Grace Houser,Paulo E. Faria Junior,Zhuohang Yu,Alessandra Ames,Gabriel A. D. Souza,Mingzu Liu,Gilmar Eugenio Marques,Leandro M. Malard,Mauricio Terrones,Vincent H. Crespi,Marcio D. Teodoro*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在钒掺杂WS₂单层中实现了谷不对称，掺杂样品的圆偏振度约为本征WS₂的两倍，DFT揭示自旋依赖杂化导致的谷间激子能量差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TMD单层可借谷自由度编码信息，但实际应用仍需对谷极化效率和谷Zeeman效应实现更优控制；近期用自旋极化金属原子作替位缺陷可打破时间反演对称性、诱导室温铁磁序并增强谷依赖光学响应，因此本文探索V掺杂WS₂中的谷不对称性。

Method: 对钒掺杂WS₂单层进行谷依赖光学测量，提取各谷的Zeeman斜率与圆偏振度；并结合密度泛函理论计算掺杂结构中的能带边位移与激子能量。

Result: 在给定磁极化下，一个谷表现出比另一个谷更大的Zeeman斜率和圆偏振度；掺杂样品的总圆偏振度约为本征WS₂单层的两倍。DFT表明自旋依赖杂化使导带边产生不同能量移动，导致两谷激子能量不同，与实验一致。

Conclusion: V掺杂WS₂单层中的谷不对称源于自旋依赖杂化引起的谷间激子能量差异，为基于缺陷工程二维材料的谷电子学技术提供了途径。

Abstract: Transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) monolayers offer an innovative platform for encoding and manipulating information through the valley degree of freedom. While unique valley-related physical phenomena have been reported so far, practical applications still require advanced control over the valley polarization efficiency and the valley Zeeman effect. Recently, the introduction of spin-polarized metal atoms as substitutional defects was reported to break the time-reversal symmetry in TMD monolayers, consequently inducing a room-temperature ferromagnetic ordering and enhancing the valley-dependent optical responses. Here, we report valley asymmetries for vanadium-doped WS$_2$ monolayers. With a given magnetic polarization, one valley exhibits larger Zeeman slope and degree of circular polarization than the other valley. Additionally, the overall degree of circular polarization in the doped samples is approximately twice that of the pristine WS$_2$ monolayer. Density functional theory calculations in the doped structure show that different energy shifts in conduction band edges due to spin-dependent hybridization lead to different exciton energies between valleys, which is consistent with the experimental observations. Our results pave the way for valleytronic technologies based on defect-engineered two-dimensional materials.

</details>


### [109] [Electrical Probing of Dark Excitons through Microwave Permittivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22710)
*Alex Stram,Zhida Liu,Ziheng Zhang,Yangchen He,Xuejian Ma,Kyoung Pyo Lee,Lisa Frammolino,Fuxiang Chen,Robert J. Boyd,Sirapas Tangton,Anand Swain,Kan Yao,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Yuebing Zheng,Chih-Kang Shih,Daniel Rhodes,Li Yang,Xiaoqin Li,Keji Lai*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用激光辐照微波阻抗显微镜，发现单层二硫化钨中光激发激子在GHz频段产生巨大、可光调控的介电响应，实现了对暗激子的无接触纳米尺度电学成像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子级薄半导体中的激子通常只能通过光学信号探测，因为其寿命极短，一般认为无法进行电学检测。作者希望突破这一限制，找到无需接触的电学探测途径，尤其是探测难以光学观测的暗激子。

Method: 采用激光辐照的微波阻抗显微镜（laser-illuminated microwave impedance microscopy）对单层二硫化钨进行测量；对比高纯度封装样品与缺陷丰富样品的介电/光电导响应；通过扩散的空间成像和亚线性功率依赖关系识别暗激子贡献；并用第一性原理计算激子极化率以验证测量到的磁化率。

Result: 高纯度封装薄片表现出与激子谱共振的纯介电响应，而缺陷丰富的样品则由传统光电导主导。空间扩散成像与亚线性功率依赖表明长寿命暗激子是主要贡献者，第一性原理计算的激子极化率与实测磁化率吻合。空间分辨率优于100 nm。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了激子作为可光调控介电元件的角色，并将微波显微镜引入为直接电学探测暗激子输运的手段，分辨率优于100 nm。

Abstract: Excitons in atomically thin semiconductors are almost always probed through their optical signatures, because the short lifetimes of these transient quasiparticles are generally assumed to preclude electrical detection. Here we show that photoexcited excitons in monolayer tungsten disulfide produce a large, optically tunable permittivity at gigahertz frequencies, and that the effect provides a contact-free electrical route to imaging dark excitons at the nanoscale. Using laser-illuminated microwave impedance microscopy, we find that high-purity encapsulated flakes exhibit a purely dielectric response resonant with the exciton spectrum, whereas defect-rich samples are governed by conventional photoconductivity. Spatial mapping of diffusion and sublinear power dependence identify long-lived dark excitons as the dominant contributors, and first-principles modelling of exciton polarizability reproduces the measured susceptibility. Our results establish excitons as optically tunable dielectric elements and introduce microwave microscopy as a direct electrical probe of dark-exciton transport with sub-100 nm resolution.

</details>


### [110] [Non-Hermitian Topology from Edge Transport in Hermitian Quantum Anomalous Hall Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23099)
*Humian Zhou,Ming Lu,Chui-Zhen Chen,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文证明全局厄米量子反常霍尔系统中手性边缘模与扩散型边缘模的相互作用可自发产生本征非互易输运，其由连续Hatano-Nelson模型描述，并在霍尔条形可观测量中直接体现非厄米趋肤效应，指出磁性拓扑绝缘体是现实平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米物理的奇异现象（如例外点、趋肤效应）以往主要在依赖受控增益与损耗的工程化系统中实现，需要外部非厄米调控。作者希望探究非厄米输运响应能否在全局厄米体系中自然涌现，从而将非厄米拓扑与介观输运联系起来。

Method: 理论分析全局厄米量子反常霍尔系统中单向手性边缘模与扩散型普通边缘模的相互作用，导出连续Hatano-Nelson模型以描述本征非互易输运；采用Landauer-Büttiker数值模拟验证输运特征，并评估磁性拓扑绝缘体作为实现平台。

Result: 电化学势与局域热耗散呈现依赖于手性的指数空间分布；纵向电导随系统尺寸指数衰减，而霍尔电导保持量子化。Landauer-Büttiker模拟确认了上述输运特征。

Conclusion: 非厄米趋肤效应可内禀地编码于可实验探测的霍尔条形可观测量中，无需外部非厄米工程；磁性拓扑绝缘体是现实平台，为固态系统中的非厄米拓扑器件开辟了路径。

Abstract: Non-Hermitian physics, known for exotic phenomena like exceptional points and the skin effect, has been most prominently realized in engineered systems relying on controlled gain and loss. Here we show that it can also arise naturally as an intrinsic transport response of a globally Hermitian quantum anomalous Hall system, without the need for external non-Hermitian engineering. We show that the interplay between unidirectional chiral edge modes and diffusive normal edge modes induces intrinsic non-reciprocal transport described by a continuum Hatano-Nelson model. Consequently, the non-Hermitian skin effect is encoded directly in experimentally accessible Hall-bar observables: the electrochemical potential and local heat dissipation acquire chirality-dependent exponential spatial profiles, while the longitudinal conductance decays exponentially with system size and the Hall conductance remains quantized. Using Landauer--B{"u}ttiker simulations, we confirm these transport signatures and identify magnetic topological insulators as a realistic platform for an intrinsic non-Hermitian transport response. Our results bridge non-Hermitian topology with mesoscopic transport, opening a pathway toward non-Hermitian topological devices in solid-state systems.

</details>


### [111] [Magnetic Quadrupole Lens and Current-Induced Quadrupolar Dynamics of Antiskyrmion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23129)
*Linfeng Du,Yang Liu,Peng Yan,Ying Su*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 反斯格明子可作为磁四极透镜聚焦/散焦电流，由此产生高度各向异性的四极反斯格明子霍尔效应，并可通过自旋轨道耦合调控；电流聚焦可显著提升反斯格明子速度并降低霍尔角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反斯格明子赛道存储器面临霍尔角过大导致器件偏离轨道的关键挑战，需要寻找新的机制来调控其电流驱动动力学。论文旨在揭示反斯格明子核心对传导电流的透镜效应及其对自身运动的反馈影响。

Method: 将量子输运计算与微磁学模拟进行迭代耦合，自洽地处理传导电子与反斯格明子的相互作用；通过自旋转移矩将电流分布与磁结构动力学耦合，并考察自旋轨道耦合的调控作用。

Result: 反斯格明子核心表现为四极透镜的焦点，在特定注入方向下电子束汇聚，形成各向异性且非均匀的电流分布；由此产生高度各向异性、可由自旋轨道耦合有效调谐的四极反斯格明子霍尔效应；电流聚焦显著提高反斯格明子速度并同时减小其霍尔角，散焦则相反。

Conclusion: 本征电流透镜效应是一种操纵拓扑磁结构的有力机制，为基于反斯格明子的自旋电子学器件（如高性能赛道存储器）开辟了新途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate that antiskyrmions can serve as magnetic quadrupole lenses that focus or defocus electric currents traversing them. We showcase the antiskyrmion core as a focal point where electron beams converge for specific injection directions, leading to anisotropic and nonuniform current distributions. Thus the current-induced antiskyrmion dynamics is profoundly modified by the magnetic quadrupole lensing via the spin-transfer torque. By incorporating quantum transport and micromagnetic simulations iteratively, we self-consistently capture the coupled dynamics of conduction electrons and antiskyrmions. Our results unveil a quadrupolar antiskyrmion Hall effect that is highly anisotropic with respect to the current direction and can be effectively tuned by the spin-orbit coupling. In particular, the current focusing (defocusing) dramatically enhances (suppresses) the antiskyrmion speed while simultaneously reducing its Hall angle, thereby overcoming a key challenge for antiskyrmion racetrack memories. Our work establishes the intrinsic current lensing as a powerful mechanism for manipulating topological magnetic textures and opens new avenues for antiskyrmion-based spintronic devices.

</details>


### [112] [Collective depinning of dipolar skyrmion chains: thermal and athermal rounding of an elastic polymer in a quenched landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23181)
*R. L. Silva,R. C. Silva,R. L. Stamps*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过随机Thiele方程数值求解，研究弱钉扎环境下相互作用偶极斯格明子链的脱钉动力学，揭示链长无关的阈值、部分热致展宽及磁场调控的渡越形貌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斯格明子链作为信息载体的器件中，链而非孤立织构的集体动力学至关重要，但相互作用链在淬火钉扎下的脱钉行为尚不清晰，需明确阈值、热效应与尺寸效应。

Method: 对含吸引性淬火钉扎的弱无序势，在不同磁场下驱动不同长度（周期性边界）的偶极斯格明子链，求解随机Thiele方程，识别临界脱钉自旋电流，并统计渡越宽度、Larkin关联长度及内部形变过程。

Result: (i) 长度≥50的链，脱钉阈值与链长无关，约为孤立斯格明子的1/3；(ii) 渡越展宽仅部分源于热效应，宽度由T=0的0.334±0.004 decade增至300 K的0.633±0.002，而本征Larkin关联长度在kBT~V0/25以下稳定约2.6键长；(iii) 宽度在kBT~0.14V0前几乎不变，至kBT~0.23V0增大约四倍，主要来自蠕变区展宽，且与链长无关，排除有限尺寸效应。磁场由30增至40 mT使渡越核心加宽26%、蠕变尾缩短。约200个斯格明子的链脱钉经由一系列内部形变：Larkin长度与粗糙度增长、瞬时键拉伸、随后迁移率与霍尔角恢复。

Conclusion: 相互作用斯格明子链的脱钉受集体效应支配，阈值与渡越宽度基本与链长无关，渡越展宽为部分热致并由蠕变区主导，磁场可调控渡越形貌；这些集体效应对于以链为信息载体的斯格明子器件具有重要意义。

Abstract: The dynamics of interacting dipole skyrmion chains in a weak pinning landscape is studied through numerical solutions of the stochastic Thiele equation. Chains of different lengths with periodic boundary conditions are driven across quenched attractive pinning sites under different magnetic fields. A clear threshold spin current is identified as the critical depinning force. Three main results emerge: (i) for chains of 50 skyrmions or more, the depinning threshold is independent of chain length and remains close to one third of that of an isolated skyrmion. (ii) The transition rounding is only partly thermal: the width changes from w = 0.334 +/- 0.004 decades at T = 0 to 0.633 +/- 0.002 at 300 K, while the intrinsic Larkin correlation length remains about 2.6 bonds below kBT ~ V0/25. (iii) The width is nearly unchanged up to kBT ~ 0.14 V0, but increases almost fourfold by kBT ~ 0.23 V0, mainly through broadening of the creep regime. It is independent of chain length, ruling out finite-size rounding. Increasing the field from 30 to 40 mT changes the transition shape, widening its core by 26% while shortening the creep tails. For chains of about 200 skyrmions, depinning proceeds through a sequence of internal deformations - growth of the Larkin length and roughness, transient bond stretching, and subsequent recovery of mobility and Hall angle. These collective effects are relevant for skyrmion-based devices in which chains, rather than isolated textures, act as information carriers.

</details>


### [113] [Non-Markovian Quantum Dynamics of Exciton-Polaritons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23295)
*Rajanya Sarkar,Pritha Ghosh,Arshath Manjalingal,David R. Reichman,Arkajit Mandal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非马尔可夫主方程方法，通过波矢空间粗粒化与密度矩阵对角元并行演化，实现了激子-极化激元非平衡动力学的全量子模拟，并在TIPS-并五苯中复现了实验观测的极化激元群速度重整化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子-极化激元在室温下展现出介观相干的量子输运，但精确的全量子动力学模拟计算代价极高，通常需借助半经典近似，从而限制了对声子、光子与电子在集体光-物质耦合下动力学相互作用的微观理解。

Method: 开发非马尔可夫主方程方法，突破传统马尔可夫极限，刻画声子诱导的退相干与耗散；对波矢空间进行粗粒化处理，并将密度矩阵各对角元并行演化，从而实现全量子模拟。以TIPS-并五苯中的激子-极化激元输运为案例进行验证。

Result: 该方法合理复现了实验观测的极化激元群速度重整化，其来源为声子诱导的非马尔可夫Lamb位移；并证明传统马尔可夫理论无法再现该重整化现象。

Conclusion: 非马尔可夫效应在激子-极化激元输运中具有关键作用，所提出的主方程方法为集体光-物质耦合体系的全量子动力学模拟提供了可行且有效的手段。

Abstract: Exciton-polaritons, hybrid light-matter quasiparticles formed when a material interacts with a confined electric field, have experimentally been shown to exhibit mesoscale coherent quantum propagation that remains robust at room temperature. However, an accurate and direct quantum dynamical simulation of this phenomenon that does not resort to semi-classical approximations is prohibitively expensive computationally, limiting the microscopic understanding of the rich dynamical interplay among phonons, photons, and electrons under collective light-matter coupling. To address this fundamental challenge, we develop a non-Markovian master equation approach which enables the fully quantum mechanical simulation of non-equilibrium exciton-polariton dynamics and captures phonon-induced decoherence and dissipation beyond the conventional Markovian limit. To carry out this task, a procedure is developed in which the wave vector space is coarse-grained and each diagonal element of the density matrix is evolved in parallel. To demonstrate the utility of this approach, we simulate exciton-polariton transport in TIPS-pentacene. We find that our approach reasonably captures the experimentally observed renormalization of the polariton group velocity, which originates from the phonon-induced non-Markovian Lamb shift. We further show that this renormalization cannot be reproduced within conventional Markovian theories.

</details>


### [114] [Non-Hermitian Quantum Mechanics I: Instantaneous Self-Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23426)
*Lingfeng Liu,Wei-Wei Yang,Jiangping Hu,Zhesen Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 从闭量子系统的幺正演化出发，严格证明整体波函数在任意局域子系统上的投影服从精确的含时非厄米薛定谔方程，其核心是瞬时自能算符，且推导不依赖玻恩近似、马尔可夫近似或宽带极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为量子系统中有效非厄米动力学的出现提供严格的微观基础，并厘清常规非厄米近似与精确描述的差异。

Method: 以闭系统幺正演化为出发点，将整体波函数投影到局域子系统上，引入含时且一般非厄米的瞬时自能算符，与子系统哈密顿量共同构成精确的含时局域生成元；推导过程不使用玻恩近似、马尔可夫近似或宽带极限等常规近似。

Result: 得到了精确的含时非厄米薛定谔方程，其中瞬时自能编码环境反作用；通过将该精确框架与常规非厄米近似进行基准对比，系统性地揭示了后者的局限。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子系统中有效非厄米动力学的涌现建立了严格的微观理论基础。

Abstract: Starting from the unitary evolution of a closed quantum system, we rigorously demonstrate that the projection of the global wavefunction onto an arbitrary local subsystem is governed by an exact, time-dependent non-Hermitian Schrödinger equation. Crucially, the derivation does not rely on conventional approximations such as the Born approximation, the Markov approximation, or the wide-band limit. The central quantity is the \textit{instantaneous self-energy}, a time-dependent and generally non-Hermitian operator that encodes environmental backaction and, together with the subsystem Hamiltonian, forms the exact time-local generator of the projected dynamics. By benchmarking the conventional non-Hermitian approximation against this exact framework, we systematically expose its limitations. These results provide a rigorous microscopic foundation for the emergence of effective non-Hermitian dynamics in quantum systems.

</details>


### [115] [Sub-Thermionic Switching in a Negative-Capacitance Bandgap-Change Field-Effect Transistor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23505)
*Rahul B Awale,Mike Klymenko,Yuefeng Yin,Nikhil V Medhekar,Bhaskaran Muralidharan,Michael S Fuhrer*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出并理论建模了负电容带隙调控场效应晶体管（NC-BCFET），利用铁电栅堆叠放大电场以打开双层石墨烯带隙，实现远超玻尔兹曼极限的亚热电子开关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破亚阈值摆幅约60 mV/dec的玻尔兹曼极限是低功耗纳电子学的核心挑战。负电容FET通过放大沟道电势来应对，但其效果受电容匹配和传统沟道量子电容的限制。

Method: 构建自洽理论框架，将四带紧束缚哈密顿量与GW修正屏蔽、准静态Landau–Devonshire铁电响应以及弹道Landauer–Büttiker输运相耦合；采用实验已验证的材料：具有电可调带隙的双层石墨烯作为沟道，Al0.55Sc0.45N作为负电容铁电体。

Result: 负电容放大了层间电势差，使开关窗口相较介电栅控对照组压缩37倍；在300 K下优化器件实现15 mV/dec的亚阈值摆幅，低于玻尔兹曼极限四倍，在100 K下进一步改善至2.5 mV/dec；室温开关比受双层石墨烯带隙限制约为10^2。

Conclusion: 该概念可直接推广至具有更大场可调带隙的材料，从而在室温下同时实现深度亚热电子开关与高开关比。

Abstract: Overcoming the approximately 60~mV/dec Boltzmann limit of the subthreshold swing remains a central challenge for low-power nanoelectronics. Negative-capacitance field-effect transistors address it by amplifying the channel potential, but their effectiveness is constrained by capacitance matching and by the quantum capacitance of conventional channels. Here we model a negative-capacitance bandgap-change field-effect transistor (NC-BCFET), in which the ferroelectric gate stack instead amplifies the electric field that opens the bandgap of the channel, using experimentally proven materials: bilayer graphene as a channel with an electrically tunable bandgap and Al$_{0.55}$Sc$_{0.45}$N as the negative-capacitance ferroelectric. Our self-consistent framework couples a four-band tight-binding Hamiltonian with GW-corrected screening, a quasistatic Landau--Devonshire ferroelectric response, and ballistic Landauer--Büttiker transport. Negative capacitance amplifies the interlayer potential difference, compressing the switching window 37-fold relative to a dielectric-gated control. At 300~K, the optimized NC-BCFET achieves a subthreshold swing of 15~mV/dec, four times below the Boltzmann limit, improving to 2.5~mV/dec at 100~K. The room-temperature on/off ratio is limited to $\sim 10^{2}$ by the bandgap of bilayer graphene. The concept extends directly to materials with larger field-tunable bandgaps, where deep subthermionic switching and high on/off ratios can be achieved together at room temperature.

</details>


### [116] [Universal Generalized Brillouin Zone Theory I: Review of the Spectral Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23523)
*Zeqi xu,Jiangping Hu,Zhesen Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本系列论文旨在建立高维非厄米系统的普适广义布里渊区（GBZ）理论。作为开篇，本文聚焦一维GBZ条件在二维失效的过渡机制，系统回顾一维谱方法并指出其不完备性，明确后续波函数方法所需解决的关键难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一维GBZ条件 |β_p|=|β_{p+1}| 在二维已知失效，但其从一维向二维逐渐过渡时如何失效尚不清楚；探究该问题揭示出一维GBZ理论本身仍不完备，需重新审视其理论根基。

Method: 系统回顾并梳理一维非厄米系统的谱方法（spectral approach）来构造GBZ，分析其推导逻辑与适用前提，定位其中的困难与缺陷所在。

Result: 明确了一维GBZ谱方法存在的根本性困难，归纳出促使采用波函数方法（wavefunction approach）的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 一维GBZ理论尚不完备，谱方法难以自然推广至高维；本文为系列第二篇论文发展波函数方法、进而建立高维普适GBZ理论奠定问题基础。

Abstract: This series of papers aims to establish a universal generalized Brillouin zone (GBZ) theory for higher-dimensional non-Hermitian systems. As the starting point of this series, we emphasize a fundamental question: while the conventional one-dimensional (1D) GBZ condition, $|β_p| = |β_{p+1}|$, is well known to fail in two dimensions, how does this breakdown actually occur as a system gradually crosses over from 1D to 2D? Investigating this question reveals that the existing 1D GBZ theory itself remains incomplete. In this first paper, we therefore systematically review the 1D spectral approach and clarify where the underlying difficulties lie. Our work identifies the key challenges that motivate the wavefunction approach developed in Paper~II.

</details>


### [117] [Multiscale Modeling of Ion Transport in Nanopores: Fitting Implicit-Water Radial Diffusion Profiles to Explicit-Water Molecular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23575)
*Mónika Valiskó,Salman Shabbir,Eszter Molnárné Lakics,Zoltán Ható,Dezső Boda*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种多尺度方法，将分子尺度的离子输运信息嵌入到计算高效的纳米孔输运模型：在NP+LEMC框架内，用径向变化的有效扩散系数拟合显式水分子动力学（MD）得到的径向电导率，并成功再现MD的径向电流分布及NaCl/CaCl₂的选择性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式水MD能提供分子尺度输运信息但计算昂贵、难以模拟器件尺度的行为；而Nernst–Planck类连续模型高效却难以刻画孔内离子迁移率的空间变化（尤其近壁处强抑制）与超出平均场的离子关联。作者希望建立二者之间的桥梁。

Method: 多尺度耦合：以显式水MD模拟获得纳米孔内的径向电导率剖面，在NP+LEMC框架（Nernst–Planck方程＋局域平衡蒙特卡洛，以计入超出平均场的离子相关性）中拟合出径向变化的有效扩散系数剖面D(r)。应用于带负电的二氧化硅纳米孔中的NaCl、CaCl₂及其混合体系。

Result: 拟合得到的扩散系数剖面能重现MD的径向电流分布，包括孔壁附近离子迁移率的强烈抑制——这是孔内空间常数扩散系数无法捕捉的。NaCl与CaCl₂输运性质定性不同：NaCl因近壁Na⁺导电增强而呈阳离子选择性；CaCl₂因强结合的Ca²⁺近表面迁移率被强烈抑制而呈弱阴离子选择性。NaCl–CaCl₂混合物中，Ca²⁺优先结合导致离子电导的非线性变化。

Conclusion: 该方法在实验相关的器件行为与计算高效的约化模型之间建立了桥梁，其中显式水MD提供可并入有效输运系数的分子尺度信息。

Abstract: We develop a multiscale approach for incorporating molecular-scale transport information into computationally efficient models of ion transport through nanopores. A radially varying effective diffusion coefficient profile is fitted to radial conductivity profiles obtained from explicit-water molecular dynamics (MD) simulations. The fitting is performed within the NP+LEMC framework, which combines the Nernst--Planck equation with Local Equilibrium Monte Carlo to account for ion correlations beyond mean-field approximation. We apply the approach to NaCl, CaCl$_2$, and their mixtures in a negatively charged silica nanopore. The resulting diffusion coefficient profiles reproduce the radial current distributions of the MD simulations, including the strong suppression of ionic mobility near the pore wall that cannot be captured by a spatially constant diffusion coefficient inside the pore. NaCl and CaCl$_2$ exhibit qualitatively different transport behavior: the former is cation selective due to enhanced near-wall Na$^+$ conduction, whereas the latter shows weak anion selectivity because strongly bound Ca$^{2+}$ ions have strongly suppressed mobility near the surface. For NaCl--CaCl$_2$ mixtures, preferential Ca$^{2+}$ binding leads to nonlinear changes in ionic conductance. The approach establishes a bridge between experimentally relevant device behavior and computationally efficient reduced models, with explicit-water MD providing the molecular-scale information that can be incorporated into the effective transport coefficients.

</details>


### [118] [Interplay of spin-orbit coupling and magnetic chirality: Multidirectional spin polarization in a helical antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23618)
*Kallol Mondal,Sudin Ganguly,Santanu K. Maiti*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在紧束缚框架下研究了含自旋轨道耦合的非共线螺旋反铁磁体中的自旋相关输运，发现螺旋磁织构与自旋轨道相互作用的共同作用可在x、y、z三个方向同时产生有限自旋极化，且长程跃迁显著增强自旋过滤效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自旋过滤系统通常只能产生单轴方向的自旋极化；本文旨在探索非共线螺旋反铁磁体中螺旋磁序与自旋轨道耦合协同作用下的自旋极化行为，以期实现多方向自旋极化。

Method: 采用紧束缚模型描述非共线螺旋反铁磁体，引入自旋轨道耦合，并利用Landauer-Büttiker形式计算自旋相关输运与自旋极化。

Result: 在螺旋磁织构与自旋轨道耦合共同作用下，可同时沿x、y、z方向产生有限自旋极化，区别于传统单轴极化；多方向自旋极化源于非共线磁序引起的对称性破缺；自旋轨道耦合显著改变自旋极化，且极化强烈依赖螺旋系统参数；长程跃迁明显增强自旋过滤效应，产生沿三个方向的大自旋极化分量。

Conclusion: 非共线螺旋反铁磁体结合自旋轨道耦合可实现多方向自旋极化，长程跃迁是增强自旋过滤的有效调控手段，为新型自旋电子器件设计提供思路。

Abstract: We investigate spin-dependent transport in a non-collinear helical antiferromagnet in the presence of spin-orbit coupling within a tight-binding framework. Using the Landauer-Büttiker formalism, we analyze the generation of spin polarization arising from the combined effects of the helical magnetic texture and spin-orbit interaction. We find that finite spin polarization can be generated simultaneously along the $\hat{x}$, $\hat{y}$, and $\hat{z}$-directions, in contrast to the predominantly single-axis spin polarization commonly observed in conventional spin-filtering systems. The multidirectional spin polarization originates from the symmetry breaking introduced by the non-collinear magnetic order. We further show that the spin polarization is modified significantly in the presence of spin-orbit coupling and it has strong dependence on the parameters associated with the helical system. In particular, long-range hopping significantly enhances the spin-filtering effect, leading to large spin polarization components along all three spatial directions.

</details>


### [119] [Scalable Multiple Electron Transport Architectures for Feedback Traceable Current Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24015)
*Agustin Javier Lapi,Guillermo Fernandez Moroni,Fernando Chierchie,Fabricio Alcalde Bessia,Miqueas Ezequiel Gamero,Brenda Aurea Cervantes Vergara,Blas Junior Irigoyen Gimenez,Eduardo Paolini,Claudio Rodrigo Chavez Blanco,Juan Estrada,Javier Tiffenberg*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出基于浮栅架构的可扩展电子计数电流单元，以多电子输运（MET）方式工作，实现离散电荷包的受控注入、输运与精确量化，并通过并联/串联多放大器架构验证噪声按独立测量数平方根降低及可编程稳定电流输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向电子可溯源电流源与精密量子计量，需要能对离散电荷包进行受控生成与精确量化的可扩展平台，并进一步降低噪声、提升电流输出的稳定性与可编程性。

Method: 采用浮栅架构的电子计数电流单元，工作于多电子输运（MET）模式；利用传感器非破坏多次读出能力实现电荷包的受控注入、输运与量化；复用电荷注入技术与电子分辨能力生成量子化电荷包；探索基于并联与串联多放大器配置的可扩展架构。

Result: 实验结果表明，噪声随独立测量数按平方根规律降低，并获得稳定、可编程的输出电流。

Conclusion: 该方案为电子计数电流源及精密量子计量应用提供了一种紧凑、可扩展的平台。

Abstract: We present a scalable electron-counting current cell based on a floating gate architecture operated as a Multiple Electron Transport device (MET). The system enables controlled injection, transport, and precise quantification of discrete charge packets using the sensor non-destructive multiple readout capability. We repurposed the charge-injection technique, jointly with the electron-resolution capability, for a controlled generation of quantized charge packets for an electron-traceable current source.
  Scalable architectures based on parallel and series multi-amplifier configurations are explored. Experimental results confirm noise reduction following the square root of the number of independent measurements and demonstrate stable, programmable output current. This approach provides a compact and scalable platform for electron-counting current sources and precision quantum metrology applications.

</details>


### [120] [The Superconducting Talbot Effect in Phased-Array Josephson Junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24572)
*Hechen Ren,Ziying Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出超导Talbot效应：近邻化库珀对在弹道二维电子气中传播时的宏观量子自成像现象，并通过相控阵约瑟夫森结与游标式探测器阵列实现主动调控与亚波长采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将光学Talbot自成像推广至超导量子体系，以实现对量子干涉波前的相干调控，并克服输运测量中的空间分辨率限制，用于费米面诊断。

Method: 将周期性超导引线构造成具有可编程相位差的相控阵约瑟夫森结，主动操控超导Talbot毯；设计游标式（Vernier-scale）探测器阵列对分数Talbot图样进行亚波长采样；并在方格子上开展紧束缚数值计算验证实空间干涉图样。

Result: 实现了超导Talbot毯的主动转向与高鲁棒性的实空间量子干涉成像，并可直接提取螺旋、自旋简并及自旋轨道劈裂费米面的费米波长；紧束缚数值计算结果与实空间干涉图样一致。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了相干波前工程与量子材料诊断的通用框架，为超导光学提供新范式。

Abstract: We introduce the superconducting Talbot effect---a macroscopic quantum self-imaging phenomenon occurring when proximitized Cooper pairs propagate through a ballistic two-dimensional electron gas. By configuring periodic superconducting leads into a phased-array Josephson junction with programmable phase differences, we demonstrate active steering of the resulting superconducting Talbot carpet. To overcome transport resolution limits, we design a Vernier-scale collector array that performs sub-wavelength sampling of the fractional Talbot pattern. This approach maps real-space quantum interference with high robustness to disorder, enabling direct extraction of Fermi wavelengths across helical, spin-degenerate, and spin-orbit-split Fermi surfaces. Tight-binding numerical calculations on a square lattice validate the real-space interference patterns. Our results establish a versatile framework for coherent wavefront engineering and quantum materials diagnostics in superconducting optics.

</details>


### [121] [Corbino-Enhanced Supersonic Acoustic-Emission Threshold in a GaAs Two-Dimensional Electron System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24585)
*A. D. Levin,A. S. Jaroshevich,Z. D. Kvon,V. A. Chitta,M. S. Aksenov,D. V. Dmitriev,A. K. Bakarov,G. M. Gusev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在高迁移率GaAs/AlGaAs二维电子气Corbino器件中测量非线性微分电阻，观察到零磁场下负电流支的极性选择阈值峰，并将其解释为类切伦科夫声子发射的局域阈值现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高迁移率电子体系中的局域非平衡电子-声子过程，并检验Corbino几何能否作为探测超音速声发射的敏感平台。

Method: 将高迁移率GaAs/AlGaAs二维电子气加工成Corbino几何，测量微分电阻R_diff随直流电流I_dc的变化，并施加垂直磁场观察其抑制效应；结合径向电流密度集中j_r(r)=I/(2πr)导致的局域电子速度超过声速进行分析。

Result: 零磁场下，R_diff(I_dc)在负电流支出现明显的极性选择阈值峰，并随垂直磁场逐渐被抑制；该异常被归因于内接触附近局域电子速度超过声速所触发的声学声子发射阈值。

Conclusion: Corbino几何是探测高迁移率电子体系中局域非平衡电子-声子过程的敏感平台，且其内接触可作为几何定义的超音速声发射源；极性选择性源于Peltier加热与冷却对接触附近局域热学与边界条件的调制，而非运动学阈值本身。

Abstract: We report nonlinear differential-resistance measurements in a high-mobility GaAs/AlGaAs two-dimensional electron system patterned in a Corbino geometry. At zero magnetic field, $R_{\mathrm{diff}}(I_{dc})$ exhibits a pronounced polarity-selective threshold peak on the negative-current branch, gradually suppressed by a perpendicular magnetic field. We interpret the Corbino anomaly as a local Cherenkov-like threshold for acoustic phonon emission, enabled by the radial current concentration $j_r(r)=I/(2πr)$, which drives the local electron velocity above the sound velocity in a region adjacent to the inner contact. The polarity selectivity is attributed to Peltier heating and cooling, which modify the local thermal and boundary conditions near the contact, rather than to the kinematic threshold itself. Our results identify the Corbino geometry as a sensitive platform for probing local nonequilibrium electron--phonon processes in a high-mobility electron system within an independently established hydrodynamic-crossover regime and suggest that the inner Corbino contact can act as a geometry-defined source for supersonic acoustic emission.

</details>


### [122] [Observation of Kondo Effect in Rhombohedral Graphene Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24734)
*Xin Liao,Qing Yin,Huiwen Wang,Jing-Wei Dong,Jun-Xi Chen,Si-Li Wu,Cai-Zhen Li,Guowei Lyu,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Wei Jiang,Yu-Gui Yao,Zhi-Min Liao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在六层菱面堆叠石墨烯莫尔超晶格中通过磁输运和变温测量实验观测到近藤效应：磁阻随磁场呈先增后减转变，霍尔电阻在临界场B_c附近符号反转；降温时R_xx先对数增长后按T^2下降，表明进入重费米液体态。该行为可由磁场或温度破坏近藤单态、释放被局域磁矩束缚的载流子来解释，并可通过载流子密度n与位移电场D连续调控，暗示近藤绝缘态的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近藤效应源于传导电子与局域磁矩间的反铁磁耦合，可产生多种奇异量子现象。二维莫尔超晶格体系具有拓扑平带，其Wannier轨道被周期性莫尔势空间局域，可作为局域磁矩，从而为观测近藤效应提供了高度可调的实验平台。

Method: 在六层菱面堆叠石墨烯莫尔超晶格中进行磁输运测量与温度依赖测量，考察磁阻、霍尔电阻R_xy随磁场的变化，以及纵向电阻R_xx随温度的演化。

Result: 随磁场增大，磁阻出现跨越临界场B_c的先增后减转变，霍尔电阻R_xy在B_c附近发生符号反转；降温时R_xx先对数增长、随后按T^2下降，表明转变为重费米液体。上述行为可一致地用磁场或温度破坏近藤单态、释放原先束缚于局域磁矩的载流子来解释。此外，近藤相互作用可由载流子密度n和位移电场D连续调控，并暗示近藤绝缘态的出现。

Conclusion: 研究结果深化了对莫尔工程平带体系中近藤效应的理解，为探索奇异的关联量子相开辟了路径。

Abstract: Kondo effect in strongly correlated systems arises from the antiferromagnetic coupling between itinerant conduction electrons and localized magnetic moments, giving rise to a variety of exotic quantum phenomena. Two-dimensional moiré superlattice systems provide a highly tunable platform featuring topological flat bands, where Wannier orbitals are spatially confined by the periodic moiré potential and serve as localized magnetic moments, enabling the observable Kondo effect. Here we experimentally demonstrate Kondo interactions in hexalayer rhombohedral graphene moiré superlattices through magneto-transport and temperature-dependent measurements. With increasing magnetic field, the magnetoresistance exhibits an increase-decrease transition across a critical field B_c, while the Hall resistance R_xy undergoes a sign reversal near B_c. Moreover, as temperature decreases, the longitudinal resistance R_xx first increases logarithmically and then decreases following a T^2 behavior, indicating a transition to heavy fermion liquid. These behaviors can be consistently explained by the breakdown of Kondo singlets induced by either magnetic field or temperature, which liberates carriers previously bound to localized moments, thereby enhancing conductivity and altering the dominant carrier type. Furthermore, our results demonstrate that the Kondo interaction can be continuously tuned by both carrier density n and displacement electric field D, and suggest the emergence of a Kondo insulating state. Our findings provide deep insight into the Kondo effect in moiré engineered flat-band systems, paving the path for exploring exotic correlated quantum phases.

</details>


### [123] [Modified Low-Temperature Scanning Tunneling Microscope for Ultrafast Pump-Probe Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24916)
*Vibhuti N. Rai,Junyoung Sim,Nils Bogdanoff,Sergey Trishin,Florian Faaber,Paul Wiechers,Caroline Firschke,Tobias Kampfrath,Christian Lotze,Katharina J. Franke*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过对Besocke"甲虫"型低温STM进行最小化改造，实现了太赫兹脉冲与光脉冲向STM结的高效耦合，并在低于6 K温度下保持原子级分辨率，成功测量了MoS₂/Au(111)的太赫兹诱导隧穿电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: THz-STM可同时实现原子级空间分辨与亚皮秒时间分辨，用于探测声子、自旋和载流子的超快动力学。但将太赫兹辐射耦合进低温STM结存在技术难题：既需激光照射的光学通道，又需维持结的稳定性。常规做法是设计全新STM头部并在超高真空腔内定制光学通路，成本与复杂度高，因此需要一种对现有设备改动最小的方案。

Method: 在仅对低温辐射屏蔽层做最小改动的前提下，改造Besocke"甲虫"式STM，而非重新设计整套STM头部。光路系统搭建在独立的激光台上，以飞秒激光脉冲在LiNbO₃晶体中产生太赫兹脉冲。通过互相关测量确定有效时间分辨率，并利用在Au(111)上外延生长的单层MoS₂测量太赫兹诱导隧穿电流。

Result: 在给定几何条件下实现了太赫兹与光脉冲向STM结的高效耦合，同时保持温度低于6 K。给出了所获得的太赫兹脉冲波形，并用互相关测量确定了有效时间分辨率。电流图呈现原子级对比度，可分辨出一个点缺陷，验证了太赫兹耦合效率与低温STM的稳定运行。

Conclusion: 表明无需全新设计STM头部，仅通过最小化改造现有低温STM即可实现有效的THz-STM测量，兼顾原子级空间分辨率与低温稳定性，为THz-STM的推广提供了低成本、可复用的改造路径。

Abstract: Combining scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) with terahertz pulses (THz-STM) enables ultrafast dynamics of phonons, spins, and charge carriers to be probed with atomic-scale spatial and sub-picosecond temporal resolution. However, coupling THz radiation into a low-temperature STM junction presents some technical challenges as it requires optical access for laser illumination while maintaining junction stability. Instead of designing a completely new STM head with customized optical access and optics within the ultra-high vacuum chamber, we modified a Besocke "Beetle" style STM with minimal changes of the cryogenic radiation shields. Despite the given geometrical conditions, we achieved efficient coupling of THz and optical pulses to the STM junction while maintaining temperatures below 6 K. We designed the optical setup on a separate laser table, starting with femtosecond laser pulses that are used to generate THz pulses in a LiNbO$_3$ crystal. We show the obtained THz pulse shapes and determine the effective time resolution using cross-correlation measurements. We further demonstrate the stable performance of the modified STM by measuring THz-induced tunneling currents from monolayer molybdenum disulfide (MoS$_2$) grown on Au(111). The resulting current maps resolve atomic-scale contrast revealing a point defect. The measurements confirm efficient THz coupling and stable low-temperature STM operation with atomic resolution.

</details>


### [124] [Model of the magnon Kerr effect in a highly anisotropic ferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24959)
*Davit Petrosyan,Hiroki Matsumoto,Hanchen Wang,Richard Schlitz,Pietro Gambardella,William Legrand*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文推导了具有单轴各向异性的铁磁薄膜中磁振子克尔效应的哈密顿量，给出不同外场方向下的克尔系数，并分析其非线性标度规律及混合腔-磁振子系统的动力学方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子克尔效应普遍存在于具有有限磁各向异性的铁磁体中，表现为磁振子色散关系随磁振子布居数变化，但目前缺乏对薄膜体系中该效应的系统理论描述及其与腔模耦合的动力学刻画。

Method: 针对单轴各向异性薄膜，推导磁振子克尔效应的哈密顿量；分别考虑外场平行、垂直及与各向异性轴成中间角度的情形，确定各自的克尔系数；分析非线性随磁振子频率与磁性样品体积的标度关系；建立含薄膜磁振子克尔非线性的混合腔-磁振子体系运动方程。

Result: 给出了三种外场取向下的克尔系数表达式，刻画了非线性在不同磁性材料中随磁振子频率和样品体积的标度行为，并由运动方程得到光子与磁振子的布居数以及腔微波透射的散射矩阵项。

Conclusion: 该工作为薄膜磁振子克尔效应提供了统一的理论框架，并将结果推广至混合腔-磁振子系统，为相关微波透射实验观测提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The magnon Kerr effect is a nonlinear phenomenon that occurs universally in any ferromagnet with finite magnetic anisotropy. It manifests itself as a magnon population-dependent change in the magnon dispersion relation. Here, we derive the Hamiltonian for the magnon Kerr effect for a thin film with uniaxial anisotropy. We consider the external field, parallel, perpendicular, and at an intermediate angle to the anisotropy axis, and determine the Kerr coefficient in each case. We show how the nonlinearity scales for different magnetic materials as a function of magnon frequency and magnetic sample volume. Moreover, we derive the equations of motion for a hybrid cavity-magnon system with such thin-film magnon Kerr nonlinearity, to obtain populations of photons and magnons, and scattering matrix terms for the microwave transmission of the cavity, relevant for experimental observations.

</details>


### [125] [Collective Coordinate Dynamics of Antiferromagnet: Gravity by Quantum Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24975)
*Kuangyin Deng,Ran Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 反铁磁与亚铁磁织构的集体坐标动力学由CP¹态量子几何决定的测地线方程支配，等价于有效黎曼引力与洛伦兹力，与无惯性的Thiele方程根本不同；应用于光驱动超快反铁磁畴壁，可解释实验观测的螺旋度相关非对称运动并预言畴壁宽度振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁磁织构常用的Thiele方程无惯性、缺乏引力类比，无法描述反铁磁与亚铁磁织构的动力学行为，亟需建立新的集体坐标理论框架。

Method: 以与Néel矢量关联的CP¹态量子几何为基础，推导反铁磁/亚铁磁织构的集体坐标动力学方程，将其表述为测地线方程，并映射为有效黎曼引力与洛伦兹力；随后应用于光驱动的超快反铁磁畴壁。

Result: 理论成功解释了实验中观测到的螺旋度相关非对称畴壁运动，并预言畴壁宽度呈现剧烈呼吸式振荡。

Conclusion: 反铁磁与亚铁磁织构的动力学具有量子几何起源的引力和规范场性质，明显区别于铁磁织构的Thiele描述，为超快自旋织构操控提供了新理论基础。

Abstract: The collective coordinate dynamics of antiferromagnetic and ferrimagnetic textures emerges as a geodesic equation governed by the quantum geometry of the CP$^1$ state associated with the Néel vector, rendering an effective Riemannian gravity and a Lorentz force in the texture motion. This unveils a physical scenario fundamentally distinct from the Thiele equation widely used for ferromagnetic textures, which is inertialess without any gravity analogy. Applying our framework to an optically driven ultrafast antiferromagnetic domain wall, we can explain the helicity-dependent asymmetric motion observed experimentally and predict a vibrantly breathing wall width.

</details>
